\eta_{3}(t)e^{i\xi_{3}(t)}\\
\eta_{4}(t)e^{i\xi_{4}(t)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\Bigg]=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&0&0&0\\
0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&0&0\\
0&0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0\\
0&0&0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
\end{pmatrix}\frac{d}{dt}\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)e^{i\xi_{1}(t)}\\
\eta_{2}(t)e^{i\xi_{2}(t)}\\
\eta_{3}(t)e^{i\xi_{3}(t)}\\
\eta_{4}(t)e^{i\xi_{4}(t)}\\
\end{pmatrix}+

\displaystyle+\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{1}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)})^{2}}&0&0&0\\
0&-\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{2}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)})^{2}}&0&0\\
0&0&-\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{3}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)})^{2}}&0\\
0&0&0&-\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{4}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)})^{2}}\\
\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)e^{i\xi_{1}(t)}\\
\eta_{2}(t)e^{i\xi_{2}(t)}\\
\eta_{3}(t)e^{i\xi_{3}(t)}\\
\eta_{4}(t)e^{i\xi_{4}(t)}\\
\end{pmatrix}=

\displaystyle\Bigg[-\frac{i}{\hbar}\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{4})&\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})\\
\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{4})&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))\\
\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})&\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{1}+\beta 2^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{4}&\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))\\
\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))&\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta 2^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{4})\\
\end{pmatrix}\times

\displaystyle\times\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&0&0&0\\
0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&0&0\\
0&0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0\\
0&0&0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
\end{pmatrix}\Bigg]\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)e^{i\xi_{1}(t)}\\
\eta_{2}(t)e^{i\xi_{2}(t)}\\
\eta_{3}(t)e^{i\xi_{3}(t)}\\
\eta_{4}(t)e^{i\xi_{4}(t)}\\
\end{pmatrix}

and we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)e^{i\xi_{1}(t)}\\
\eta_{2}(t)e^{i\xi_{2}(t)}\\
\eta_{3}(t)e^{i\xi_{3}(t)}\\
\eta_{4}(t)e^{i\xi_{4}(t)}\\
\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{1}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)})}&0&0&0\\
0&\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{2}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)})}&0&0\\
0&0&\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{3}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)})}&0\\
0&0&0&\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{4}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)})}\\
\end{pmatrix}+(-\frac{i}{\hbar})\begin{pmatrix}H_{w11}&H_{w12}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&H_{w13}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&H_{w14}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
H_{w21}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&H_{w22}&H_{w23}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&H_{w24}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
H_{w31}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&H_{w32}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&H_{w33}&H_{w34}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
H_{w41}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&H_{w42}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&H_{w43}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&H_{w44}\\
\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)e^{i\xi_{1}(t)}\\
\eta_{2}(t)e^{i\xi_{2}(t)}\\
\eta_{3}(t)e^{i\xi_{3}(t)}\\
\eta_{4}(t)e^{i\xi_{4}(t)}\\
\end{pmatrix}.

Next step allows for identification of 8 probabilities embedded among 4 dimensional state vector in the way as given below

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)e^{i\xi_{1}(t)}=\eta_{1}(t)cos(\xi_{1}(t))+i\eta_{1}(t)sin(\xi_{1}(t))=P_{1re}(t)+iP_{1im}(t)\\
\eta_{2}(t)e^{i\xi_{2}(t)}=\eta_{2}(t)cos(\xi_{2}(t))+i\eta_{2}(t)sin(\xi_{2}(t))=P_{2re}(t)+iP_{2im}(t)\\
\eta_{3}(t)e^{i\xi_{3}(t)}=\eta_{3}(t)cos(\xi_{3}(t))+i\eta_{3}(t)sin(\xi_{3}(t))=P_{3re}(t)+iP_{3im}(t)\\
\eta_{4}(t)e^{i\xi_{4}(t)}=\eta_{4}(t)cos(\xi_{4}(t))+i\eta_{4}(t)sin(\xi_{4}(t))=P_{4re}(t)+iP_{4im}(t)\\
\end{pmatrix}=(-\frac{i}{\hbar})\begin{pmatrix}H_{w11}+\hbar i\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{1}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)})}&H_{w12}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&H_{w13}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&H_{w14}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
H_{w21}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&H_{w22}+\hbar i\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{2}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)})}&H_{w23}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&H_{w24}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
H_{w31}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&H_{w32}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&H_{w33}+\hbar i\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{3}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)})}&H_{w34}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
H_{w41}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&H_{w42}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&H_{w43}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&H_{w44}+\hbar i\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{4}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)})}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\times\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)e^{i\xi_{1}(t)}=\eta_{1}(t)cos(\xi_{1}(t))+i\eta_{1}(t)sin(\xi_{1}(t))=P_{1re}(t)+iP_{1im}(t),\\
\eta_{2}(t)e^{i\xi_{2}(t)}=\eta_{2}(t)cos(\xi_{2}(t))+i\eta_{2}(t)sin(\xi_{2}(t))=P_{2re}(t)+iP_{2im}(t),\\
\eta_{3}(t)e^{i\xi_{3}(t)}=\eta_{3}(t)cos(\xi_{3}(t))+i\eta_{3}(t)sin(\xi_{3}(t))=P_{3re}(t)+iP_{3im}(t),\\
\eta_{4}(t)e^{i\xi_{4}(t)}=\eta_{4}(t)cos(\xi_{4}(t))+i\eta_{4}(t)sin(\xi_{4}(t))=P_{4re}(t)+iP_{4im}(t),\\
\end{pmatrix},

We can redefine the last equation system and equivalently obtain vector of quasi-probabilities as given by
P_{\textbf{k}re}(t)=\eta_{\textbf{k}}(t)cos(\xi_{\textbf{k}}(t))
,
P_{\textbf{k}im}(t)=\eta_{\textbf{k}}(t)sin(\xi_{\textbf{k}}(t))
for
k=1..4
so, it leads to the following equations of motion.

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\begin{pmatrix}P_{1re}(t)\\
P_{1im}(t)\\
P_{2re}(t)\\
P_{2im}(t)\\
P_{3re}(t)\\
P_{3im}(t)\\
P_{4re}(t)\\
P_{4im}(t)\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{1}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)})}&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w11}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w12}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w13}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w14}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w11}&-\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{1}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)})}&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w12}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w13}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w14}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}&0\\
0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w21}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{2}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)})}&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w22}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w23}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w24}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w21}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w22}&-\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{2}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)})}&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w23}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w24}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}&0\\
0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w31}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w32}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{3}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)})}&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w33}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w34}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w31}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w32}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w33}&-\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{3}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)})}&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w34}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}&0\\
0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w41}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w42}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w43}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{4}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)})}&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w44}\\
-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w41}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w42}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w43}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w44}&-\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{4}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)})}\\
\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}P_{1re}(t)\\

P_{2re}(t)\\
P_{2im}(t)\\
P_{3re}(t)\\
P_{3im}(t)\\

\end{pmatrix}

Here we have used
\eta_{k}(t)=\sqrt{(P_{\textbf{k}re}(t))^{2}+(P_{\textbf{k}im}(t))^{2}}
with
k=1..4
.
We can redefine
P_{kre}(t)\in(-1,+1)
and
P_{kim}(t)\in(-1,+1)
, so
1+P_{kre}(t)=P_{krep}(t)\in(0,+1)
and
1+P_{kim}(t)=P_{kimp}(t)\in(0,+1)
, so we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\begin{pmatrix}P_{1rep}(t)\\
P_{1imp}(t)\\
P_{2rep}(t)\\
P_{2imp}(t)\\
P_{3rep}(t)\\
P_{3imp}(t)\\
P_{4rep}(t)\\
P_{4imp}(t)\\
\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{1}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)})}&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w11}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w12}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w13}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w14}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w11}&-\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{1}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)})}&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w12}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w13}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w14}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}&0\\
0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w21}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{2}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)})}&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w22}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w23}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w24}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\
-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w21}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w22}&-\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{2}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)})}&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w23}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w24}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}&0\\
0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w31}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w32}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{3}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)})}&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w33}&0&\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w34}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\\

-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w41}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w42}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w43}\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}&0&-\frac{1}{\hbar}H_{w44}&-\frac{\frac{d}{dt}(\eta_{4}(t))}{2(\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)})}\\
\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}P_{1rep}(t)-1\\

P_{2imp}(t)-1\\
P_{3rep}(t)-1\\

P_{4imp}(t)-1\\

Here we have used
\eta_{k}(t)=\sqrt{(P_{\textbf{k}rep}(t)-1)^{2}+(P_{\textbf{k}imp}(t)-1)^{2}}
with
k=1..4
.
We recognize that the equation for stochastic Finite State Machine has the generalized form of epidemic model equation

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\vec{P}(t)=\hat{M}_{t}\vec{P}(t)+\vec{b}([\vec{P}]).

(160)

However here
P_{\textbf{k}rep}(t)
,
P_{\textbf{k}imp}(t)
are positive-value and are not normalized, so one needs to renormalize it by
assigning the probability by
\frac{P_{\textbf{k}rep}(t)}{P_{\textbf{1}rep}(t)+P_{\textbf{1}imp}(t)+P_{\textbf{2}rep}(t)+P_{\textbf{2}imp}(t)+P_{\textbf{3}rep}(t)+P_{\textbf{3}imp}(t)+P_{\textbf{4}rep}(t)+P_{\textbf{4}imp}(t)+P_{\textbf{5}rep}(t)+P_{\textbf{5}imp}(t)+P_{\textbf{6}rep}(t)+P_{\textbf{6}imp}(t)+P_{\textbf{7}rep}(t)+P_{\textbf{7}imp}(t)+P_{\textbf{8}rep}(t)+P_{\textbf{8}imp}(t)}
.

Furthermore one can obtain alternative derivation of finite stochastic machine model from tight-binding model. We have

\displaystyle i\hbar\frac{d}{dt}\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}e^{i\xi_{1}(t)},\\
\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}e^{i\xi_{2}(t)},\\
\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}e^{i\xi_{3}(t)},\\
\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}e^{i\xi_{4}(t)},\\
\end{pmatrix}=-i\hbar\frac{1}{2}\begin{pmatrix}\frac{(\frac{d}{dt}\eta_{1}(t))}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}e^{i\xi_{1}(t)},\\
\frac{(\frac{d}{dt}\eta_{2}(t))}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}e^{i\xi_{2}(t)},\\
\frac{(\frac{d}{dt}\eta_{3}(t))}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}e^{i\xi_{3}(t)},\\
\frac{(\frac{d}{dt}\eta_{4}(t))}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}e^{i\xi_{4}(t)},\\
\end{pmatrix}+i\hbar\begin{pmatrix}i\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}e^{i\xi_{1}(t)}\frac{d}{dt}\xi_{1}(t),\\
i\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}e^{i\xi_{2}(t)}\frac{d}{dt}\xi_{2}(t),\\
i\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}e^{i\xi_{3}(t)}\frac{d}{dt}\xi_{3}(t),\\
i\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}e^{i\xi_{4}(t)}\frac{d}{dt}\xi_{4}(t),\\
\end{pmatrix}=

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{4})&\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})\\
\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{4})&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))\\
\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})&\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{1}+\beta 2^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{4}&\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))\\
\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))&\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta 2^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{4})\\
\end{pmatrix}\times

\displaystyle\times\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}e^{+i\xi_{1}(t)}&0&0&0\\
0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}e^{+i\xi_{2}(t)}&0&0\\
0&0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}e^{+i\xi_{3}(t)}&0\\
0&0&0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}e^{+i\xi_{4}(t)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}e^{-i\xi_{1}(t)}&0&0&0\\
0&\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}e^{-i\xi_{2}(t)}&0&0\\
0&0&\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}e^{-i\xi_{3}(t)}&0\\
0&0&0&\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}e^{-i\xi_{4}(t)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}e^{i\xi_{1}(t)},\\
\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}e^{i\xi_{2}(t)},\\
\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}e^{i\xi_{3}(t)},\\
\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}e^{i\xi_{4}(t)},\\
\end{pmatrix}

Further simplification gives

\displaystyle\Bigg[i\hbar\frac{1}{2}\begin{pmatrix}e^{i\xi_{1}(t)}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}&0&0&0\\

0&0&0&e^{i\xi_{4}(t)}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}\frac{d}{dt}\xi_{4}(t)\\
\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)\\
\eta_{2}(t)\\
\eta_{3}(t)\\
\eta_{4}(t)\\
\end{pmatrix}\Bigg]=

\displaystyle\Bigg[\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{4})&\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})\\
\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{4})&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))\\
\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})&\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{1}+\beta 2^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{4}&\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))\\
\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})&\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))&\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))&\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta 2^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{4})\\
\end{pmatrix}\times

\displaystyle\times\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}e^{+i\xi_{1}(t)}&0&0&0\\
0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}e^{+i\xi_{2}(t)}&0&0\\
0&0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}e^{+i\xi_{3}(t)}&0\\
0&0&0&\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}e^{+i\xi_{4}(t)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\Bigg]\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)=P_{1}(t)\\
\eta_{2}(t)=P_{2}(t)\\
\eta_{3}(t)=P_{3}(t)\\
\eta_{4}(t)=P_{4}(t)\\
\end{pmatrix}.

and consequently we arrive to

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)\\
\eta_{2}(t)\\
\eta_{3}(t)\\
\eta_{4}(t)\\
\end{pmatrix}=-\frac{2i}{\hbar}\begin{pmatrix}[\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{4})+\hbar\frac{d}{dt}\xi_{1}(t)]&\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{2}(t)-\xi_{1}(t))}[\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))]&\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{3}(t)-\xi_{1}(t))}[\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))]&\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{4}(t)-\xi_{1}(t))}[\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})]\\
\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{1}(t)-\xi_{2}(t))}[\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))]&[\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{4})+\hbar\frac{d}{dt}\xi_{2}(t)]&\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{3}(t)-\xi_{2}(t))}[\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})]&\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{4}(t)-\xi_{2}(t))}[\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))]\\
\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{1}(t)-\xi_{3}(t))}[\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))]&\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{2}(t)-\xi_{3}(t))}[\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})]&[\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{1}+\beta 2^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{2}^{2}E_{4}+\hbar\frac{d}{dt}\xi_{3}(t)]&\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{4}(t)-\xi_{3}(t))}[\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))]\\
\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{1}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{1}(t)-\xi_{4}(t))}[\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(E_{1}-E_{2}-E_{3}+E_{4})]&\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{2}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{2}(t)-\xi_{4}(t))}[\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{3})+\beta_{1}^{2}(-E_{2}+E_{4}))]&\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{4}(t)}}{\sqrt{\eta_{3}(t)}}e^{+i(\xi_{3}(t)-\xi_{4}(t))}[\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(\alpha_{2}^{2}(-E_{1}+E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(-E_{3}+E_{4}))]&[\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}E_{1}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{2})+\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta 2^{2}E_{3}+\beta_{1}^{2}E_{4})+\hbar\frac{d}{dt}\xi_{4}(t)]\\
\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)\\
\eta_{2}(t)\\
\eta_{3}(t)\\
\eta_{4}(t)\\
\end{pmatrix}=

\displaystyle=(E_{val1}\left|St1(t)\right\rangle\left\langle St1(t)\right|+E_{val2}\left|St2(t)\right\rangle\left\langle St2(t)\right|+E_{val3}\left|St3(t)\right\rangle\left\langle St3(t)\right|+E_{val4}\left|St4(t)\right\rangle\left\langle St4(t)\right|)

\displaystyle(p_{v1}e^{\int_{t_{0}}^{t}E_{val1}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}}\left|St1(t)\right\rangle+p_{v2}e^{\int_{t_{0}}^{t}E_{val2}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}}\left|St2(t)\right\rangle+p_{v3}e^{\int_{t_{0}}^{t}E_{val3}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}}\left|St3(t)\right\rangle+p_{v4}e^{\int_{t_{0}}^{t}E_{val4}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}}\left|St4(t)\right\rangle)=

\displaystyle=(E_{val1}(t)p_{v1}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})]]e^{\int_{t_{0}}^{t}E_{val1}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}}\left|St1(t)\right\rangle+E_{val2}(t)p_{v2}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})]e^{\int_{t_{0}}^{t}E_{val2}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}}\left|St2(t)\right\rangle+

\displaystyle+E_{val3}(t)p_{v3}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})]e^{\int_{t_{0}}^{t}E_{val3}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}}\left|St3(t)\right\rangle+E_{val4}(t)p_{v4}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})]e^{\int_{t_{0}}^{t}E_{val4}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}}\left|St4(t)\right\rangle),

(162)

where

\displaystyle\left|\psi(t)\right\rangle_{class}=\begin{pmatrix}\eta_{1}(t)\\
\eta_{2}(t)\\
\eta_{3}(t)\\
\eta_{4}(t)\\
\end{pmatrix}=(p_{v1}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})]e^{\int_{t_{0}}^{t}E_{val1}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}}\left|St1(t)\right\rangle+p_{v2}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})]e^{E_{val2}t}\left|St2(t)\right\rangle+

\displaystyle+p_{v3}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})]e^{\int_{t_{0}}^{t}E_{val3}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}}\left|St3(t)\right\rangle+p_{v4}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})]e^{\int_{t_{0}}^{t}E_{val4}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}}\left|St4(t)\right\rangle)

(163)

and we have

\displaystyle\left\langle St1(t_{0})\right|\left|\psi(t_{0})\right\rangle_{class}=p_{v1}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})],\left\langle St2(t_{0})\right|\left|\psi(t_{0})\right\rangle_{class}=p_{v2}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})],

\displaystyle\left\langle St3(t_{0})\right|\left|\psi(t_{0})\right\rangle_{class}=p_{v3}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})],\left\langle St4(t_{0})\right|\left|\psi(t_{0})\right\rangle_{class}=p_{v4}[\eta_{1}(t_{0}),\eta_{2}(t_{0}),\eta_{3}(t_{0}),\eta_{4}(t_{0})],

We obtain explicit diagonal values of Hamiltonian in Wannier representation given as

\displaystyle H_{w11}=\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}H_{11}+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{12}+H_{21})+\beta_{2}^{2}H_{22})+\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(H_{1,3}+H_{3,1})+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{1,4}+H_{2,3}+H_{3,2}+H_{4,1})+\beta_{2}^{2}(H_{2,4}+H_{4,2}))+

\displaystyle\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}H_{3,3}+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{3,4}+H_{4,3})+\beta_{2}^{2}H_{4,4})

(165)

\displaystyle H_{w22}=\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}H_{11}-\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{12}+H_{21})+\beta_{1}^{2}H_{22})+\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}(H_{13}+H_{31})-\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{14}+H_{23}+H_{32}+H_{41})+\beta_{1}^{2}(H_{24}+H_{42}))+

\displaystyle\alpha_{2}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}H_{33}-\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{34}+H_{43})+\beta_{1}^{2}H_{44})

(166)

\displaystyle H_{w33}=\alpha 2^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}H_{11}+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{12}+H21)+\beta_{2}^{2}H_{22})-\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(H_{13}+H_{31})+

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{14}+H_{23}+H_{32}+H_{41})+\beta_{2}^{2}(H_{24}+H_{42}))+\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}H_{33}+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{34}+H_{43})+\beta_{2}^{2}H_{44})

(167)

\displaystyle H_{w44}=\alpha 2^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}H_{11}-\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{12}+H_{21})+\beta_{1}^{2}H_{22})+

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(-\beta_{2}^{2}(H_{13}+H_{31})+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{14}+H_{23}+H_{32}+H_{41})-\beta_{1}^{2}(H24+H42))+\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{2}^{2}H_{33}-\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(H_{34}+H_{43})+\beta_{1}^{2}H_{44})

(168)

and values of diagonal given as

\displaystyle H_{w12}=\alpha_{1}^{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}H_{12}-\beta_{2}^{2}H_{21}+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(-H_{11}+H_{22}))++\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(H_{14}+H_{32})-\beta_{2}^{2}(H_{23}+H_{41})+

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(\beta_{1}^{2}(H_{14}+H_{32})-\beta_{2}^{2}(H_{23}+H_{41})+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(-H_{13}+H_{24}-H_{31}+H_{42}))+

\displaystyle+\alpha_{2}^{2}(-\beta_{2}^{2}H_{34}+\beta_{1}^{2}H_{43}+\beta_{1}\beta 2(-H_{33}+H_{44}))

(169)

\displaystyle H_{w21}=\alpha_{1}^{2}(-\beta_{2}^{2}H_{12}+\beta 1^{2}H_{21}+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(-H_{11}+H_{22}))+\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}(-\beta_{2}^{2}(H_{14}+H_{32})+\beta_{1}^{2}(H_{23}+H_{41})+

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(-H_{13}+H_{24}-H_{31}+H_{42}))+\alpha_{2}^{2}(-\beta_{2}^{2}H_{34}+\beta_{1}^{2}H_{43}+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}(-H_{33}+H_{44})).

(170)

Wannier transformation and its inverse for 4 energy level quantum system is expressed as tensor product of two matrices given as

\displaystyle\hat{W}^{-1}=\begin{pmatrix}+\alpha_{1}&-\alpha_{2}\\
+\alpha_{2}&+\alpha_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\times\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&-\beta_{2}\\
+\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}+\alpha_{1}\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&-\beta_{2}\\
+\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}&-\alpha_{2}\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&-\beta_{2}\\
+\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\
+\alpha_{2}\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&-\beta_{2}\\
+\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}&+\alpha_{1}\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&-\beta_{2}\\
+\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\
\end{pmatrix}=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}=+u_{1}&-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}=+u_{2}&-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}=+u_{3}&+\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}=+u_{4}\\
+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}=-u_{2}&+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}=+u_{1}&-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}=-u_{4}&-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}=+u_{3}\\
+\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}=-u_{3}&-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}=-u_{4}&+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}=+u_{1}&-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}=+u_{2}\\
+\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}=+u_{4}&+\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}=-u_{3}&+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}=-u_{2}&+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}=+u_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}+u_{1}&+u_{2}&+u_{3}&+u_{4}\\
-u_{2}&+u_{1}&-u_{4}&+u_{3}\\
-u_{3}&-u_{4}&+u_{1}&+u_{2}\\
+u_{4}&-u_{3}&-u_{2}&+u_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle\hat{W}=\begin{pmatrix}+\alpha_{1}&+\alpha_{2}\\
-\alpha_{2}&+\alpha_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\times\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&+\beta_{2}\\
-\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}+\alpha_{1}\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&+\beta_{2}\\
-\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}&\alpha_{2}\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&+\beta_{2}\\
-\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\
-\alpha_{2}\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&+\beta_{2}\\
-\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}&+\alpha_{1}\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&+\beta_{2}\\
-\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\\
\end{pmatrix}=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}=+u_{1}&+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}=-u_{2}&+\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}=-u_{3}&+\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}=+u_{4}\\
-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}=+u_{2}&+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}=+u_{1}&-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}=-u_{4}&+\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}=-u_{3}\\
-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}=+u_{3}&-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}=-u_{4}&+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}=+u_{1}&+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}=-u_{2}\\
+\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}=+u_{4}&-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}=+u_{3}&-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}=-u_{2}&+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}=+u_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}+u_{1}&-u_{2}&-u_{3}&+u_{4}\\
+u_{2}&+u_{1}&-u_{4}&-u_{3}\\
+u_{3}&-u_{4}&+u_{1}&-u_{2}\\
+u_{4}&+u_{3}&-u_{2}&+u_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle|\alpha_{1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{2}|^{2}=1,|\beta_{1}|^{2}+|\beta_{2}|^{2}=1,|u_{1}|^{2}+|u_{2}|^{2}+|u_{3}|^{2}+|u_{4}|^{2}=1

The condition
|u_{1}|^{2}+|u_{2}|^{2}+|u_{3}|^{2}+|u_{4}|^{2}=1
is equivalent to unit sphere in 4-dimensional Euclidean space.
Such reasoning for determination of Wannier transformation can be conduced for quantum system with
2^{n}
energies.
For example one can obtain Wannier transformation for
2^{3}=8
energy levels with predefined structure given as

\displaystyle\hat{W}_{8}=\begin{pmatrix}+\alpha_{1}&+\alpha_{2}\\
-\alpha_{2}&+\alpha_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\times\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&+\beta_{2}\\
-\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\times\begin{pmatrix}+\gamma_{1}&+\gamma_{2}\\
-\gamma_{2}&+\gamma_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle\hat{W}_{8}^{-1}=\begin{pmatrix}+\alpha_{1}&-\alpha_{2}\\
+\alpha_{2}&+\alpha_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\times\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&-\beta_{2}\\
+\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\times\begin{pmatrix}+\gamma_{1}&-\gamma_{2}\\
+\gamma_{2}&+\gamma_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle|\alpha_{1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{2}|^{2}=1,|\beta_{1}|^{2}+|\beta_{2}|^{2}=1,|\gamma_{1}|^{2}+|\gamma_{2}|^{2}=1,

(172)

Having quantum system with 4 eigenergies we are given normalization condition for its eigenergy wavefunctions that is formally expressed as

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}dx\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}dy|\psi_{E1}(x,y)|^{2}=1,\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}dx\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}dy|\psi_{E2}(x,y)|^{2}=1,

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}dx\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}dy|\psi_{E3}(x,y)|^{2}=1,\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}dx\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}dy|\psi_{E4}(x,y)|^{2}=1.

(173)

what implies that 4 unique orthonormal Wannier functions referring to Fig.
5
. can be identified as linear transformation of eigenergy wavefunctions, so

\displaystyle w_{Upper,L}(x,y)=\alpha_{1}\psi_{E1}(x,y)+\alpha_{2}\psi_{E2}(x,y)+\alpha_{3}\psi_{E3}(x,y)+\alpha_{4}\psi_{E4}(x,y),

\displaystyle w_{Upper,R}(x,y)=\beta_{1}\psi_{E1}(x,y)+\beta_{2}\psi_{E2}(x,y)+\beta_{3}\psi_{E3}(x,y)+\beta_{4}\psi_{E4}(x,y),

\displaystyle w_{Lower,L}(x,y)=\gamma_{1}\psi_{E1}(x,y)+\gamma_{2}\psi_{E2}(x,y)+\gamma_{3}\psi_{E3}(x,y)+\gamma_{4}\psi_{E4}(x,y),

\displaystyle w_{Lower,R}(x,y)=\eta_{1}\psi_{E1}(x,y)+\eta_{2}\psi_{E2}(x,y)+\eta_{3}\psi_{E3}(x,y)+\eta_{4}\psi_{E4}(x,y).

In bra-ket notation intoduced by Dirac
[
3
]
we one can write
\left|\vec{w}\right\rangle=\hat{W}\left|\vec{E_{k}}\right\rangle

and we have 4 possible solutions for the system with 4 eigenergies

\displaystyle w_{1}(x,y)=w_{Upper,Left}(x,y)=+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\psi_{E1}(x,y)+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\psi_{E2}(x,y)+\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\psi_{E3}(x,y)+\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\psi_{E4}(x,y),

\displaystyle w_{2}(x,y)=w_{Upper,Right}(x,y)=-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\psi_{E1}(x,y)+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\psi_{E2}(x,y)-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\psi_{E3}(x,y)+\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\psi_{E4}(x,y),

\displaystyle w_{3}(x,y)=w_{Lower,Left}(x,y)=-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\psi_{E1}(x,y)-\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\psi_{E2}(x,y)+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\psi_{E3}(x,y)+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\psi_{E4}(x,y),

\displaystyle w_{4}(x,y)=w_{Lower,Right}(x,y)=+\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\psi_{E1}(x,y)-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}\psi_{E2}(x,y)-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}\psi_{E3}(x,y)+\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\psi_{E4}(x,y),

Alternatively we can write inverse Wannier transform in the form as
\hat{W}^{-1}\left|\vec{w}\right\rangle=\left|\vec{E_{k}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\left\langle x,y\right|\hat{W}^{-1}\left|\vec{w}\right\rangle=\Bigg[\begin{pmatrix}+\alpha_{1}&-\alpha_{2}\\
+\alpha_{2}&+\alpha_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\times\begin{pmatrix}+\beta_{1}&-\beta_{2}\\
+\beta_{2}&+\beta_{1}\\
\end{pmatrix}\Bigg]\begin{pmatrix}\psi_{w1}(x,y)\\
\psi_{w2}(x,y)\\
\psi_{w3}(x,y)\\
\psi_{w4}(x,y)\\
\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\psi_{E1}(x,y)\\
\psi_{E2}(x,y)\\
\psi_{E3}(x,y)\\
\psi_{E4}(x,y)\\
\end{pmatrix}=\left\langle x,y\right|\left|\vec{E_{k}}\right\rangle.

Basing on normalization condition for Wannier functions we can set
\alpha_{1}=cos(\Theta_{1})
,
\alpha_{2}=sin(\Theta_{1})
,
\beta_{1}=cos(\Theta_{2})
,
\beta_{2}=sin(\Theta_{2})
.
Now we need to determine coefficients in Wannier transformation as by usage of fact that Wannier functions are maximum localized in certain geometrical regions what is formally expressed by the conditions

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{0}dx\int_{0}^{\infty}dy|w_{1}(x,y)|^{2}dxdy=f_{1}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2}),\frac{d}{d\Theta_{1}}f_{1}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})=0,\frac{d}{d\Theta_{2}}f_{1}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})=0,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}dx\int_{0}^{\infty}dy|w_{2}(x,y)|^{2}dxdy=f_{2}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2}),\frac{d}{d\Theta_{1}}f_{2}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})=0,\frac{d}{d\Theta_{2}}f_{2}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})=0

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{0}dx\int_{-\infty}^{0}dy|w_{3}(x,y)|^{2}dxdy=f_{3}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2}),\frac{d}{d\Theta_{1}}f_{3}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})=0,\frac{d}{d\Theta_{2}}f_{3}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})=0

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}dx\int_{-\infty}^{0}dy|w_{4}(x,y)|^{2}dxdy=f_{4}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2}),\frac{d}{d\Theta_{1}}f_{4}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})=0,\frac{d}{d\Theta_{2}}f_{4}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})=0,

\displaystyle 0=\int_{-\infty}^{0}dx\int_{0}^{\infty}dyw_{1}^{*}(x,y)w_{2}(x,y)dxdy,0=\int_{-\infty}^{0}dx\int_{0}^{\infty}dyw_{1}^{*}(x,y)w_{3}(x,y)dxdy,

\displaystyle 0=\int_{-\infty}^{0}dx\int_{0}^{\infty}dyw_{1}^{*}(x,y)w_{4}(x,y)dxdy,0=\int_{-\infty}^{0}dx\int_{0}^{\infty}dyw_{3}^{*}(x,y)w_{2}(x,y)dxdy

\displaystyle 0=\int_{-\infty}^{0}dx\int_{0}^{\infty}dyw_{2}^{*}(x,y)w_{4}(x,y)dxdy,0=\int_{-\infty}^{0}dx\int_{0}^{\infty}dyw_{3}^{*}(x,y)w_{4}(x,y)dxdy

(176)

indicating maxima with respect to
\Theta_{1}
and
\Theta_{2}
parameters, so consequently fractional derivatives shall be equal to zero .
Particular condition
\frac{1}{cos(\Theta_{1})^{2}cos(\Theta_{2})^{2}}\frac{d}{d\Theta_{1}}f_{1}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})=0
implies

\displaystyle-2tan(\Theta_{1})\gamma_{1,1}-2tan(\Theta_{1})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{1,2}+

\displaystyle+(1-tan(\Theta_{1})^{2})\gamma_{1,3}+(1-tan(\Theta_{1})^{2})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{1,4}+

\displaystyle-2tan(\Theta_{1})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{2,1}-2tan(\Theta_{1})tan(\Theta_{2})^{2}\gamma_{2,2}+

\displaystyle+(1-tan(\Theta_{1})^{2})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{2,3}+(1-tan(\Theta_{1})^{2})tan(\Theta_{2})^{2}\gamma_{2,4}+

\displaystyle+(1-tan(\Theta_{1})^{2})\gamma_{3,1}+(1-tan(\Theta_{1})^{2})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{3,2}+

\displaystyle+(1-tan(\Theta_{1})^{2})\gamma_{3,3}+2tan(\Theta_{1})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{3,4}+

\displaystyle+(1-tan(\Theta_{1})^{2})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{4,1}+(1-tan(\Theta_{1})^{2})tan(\Theta_{2})^{2}\gamma_{4,2}+

\displaystyle+2tan(\Theta_{1})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{4,3}+2tan(\Theta_{1})^{2}tan(\Theta_{2})^{2}\gamma_{4,4}

(177)

and condition

\frac{1}{cos(\Theta_{1})^{2}cos(\Theta_{2})^{2}}\frac{d}{d\Theta_{2}}f_{1}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})=0

implies

\displaystyle-2tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{1,1}+(1-tan(\Theta_{2})^{2})\gamma_{1,2}+

\displaystyle-2tan(\Theta_{1})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{1,3}+tan(\Theta_{1})(1-tan(\Theta_{2})^{2})\gamma_{1,4}+

\displaystyle+(1-tan(\Theta_{2})^{2})\gamma_{2,1}-2tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{2,2}+

\displaystyle+tan(\Theta_{1})(1-tan(\Theta_{2})^{2})\gamma_{2,3}+2tan(\Theta_{1})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{2,4}+

\displaystyle-2tan(\Theta_{1})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{3,1}+tan(\Theta_{1})(1-(tan(\Theta_{2}))^{2})\gamma_{3,2}+

\displaystyle-2tan(\Theta_{1})^{2}tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{3,3}+tan(\Theta_{1})^{2}(1-tan(\Theta_{2})^{2})\gamma_{3,4}+

\displaystyle+tan(\Theta_{1})(1-tan(\Theta_{2})^{2})\gamma_{4,1}+2tan(\Theta_{1})tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{4,2}+

\displaystyle+tan(\Theta_{1})^{2}(1-tan(\Theta_{2})^{2})\gamma_{4,3}+2tan(\Theta_{1})^{2}tan(\Theta_{2})\gamma_{4,4}

(178)

We obtain two quadrature equations for
tan(\Theta_{1})
and
tan(\Theta_{2})
that have analytical solutions.

XV
Conclusions

The obtained results shows that quantum mechanical phenomena might be almost entirely simulated by classical statistical model encoded in epidemic model or in more general form in stochastic Finite State Machine. It includes the quantum like entanglement and superposition of states. Therefore coupled epidemic models expressed by classical systems in terms of classical physics can be the base for possible incorporation of quantum technologies and in particular
for quantum like computation and quantum like communication. In the conduced computations Wolfram software was used
[
29
]
. All work presented at
[
14
]
,

[
24
]
can be expressed by classical epidemic model. It is expected that time crystals can be also described in the given framework
[
25
]
,
[
26
]
.
It is open issue to what extent we can parameterize various condensed matter phenomena by stochastic finite state machine
[
18
]
.
The main achievements can be characterized as

•

Position-based qubit with external source of vector potential can fully reproduce the behavior of linear and non-linear epidemic model.

•

Entanglement in classical and quantum systems were identified.

•

Density matrix was formulated for classical and quantum system.

•

Entanglement entropy was formulated for classical and quantum system.

•

Dissipation was identified in classical and in quantum system as non-fundamental description of system dynamics.

and will be continued in next papers.
Various other measurements of classical entanglement can be proposed mainly basing on already existing measurements of quantum entropy
[
23
]
.
Similar way of reasoning was also expressed by
[
22
]
. Various types of quantum gates
[
31
]
can be implemented by usage of classical statistical model. One can also tackle with problem of entanglement of matter and radiation
[
19
]
,

[
20
]
,
[
27
]
,
[
28
]
that is known in quantum mechanics
so it can be expressed by classical epidemic model. One shall expect that phenomena as quantum time crystals
[
25
]
,
[
26
]
or movement of electron in crystal
[
18
]
can also be represented effectively by classical epidemic model or stochastic Finite State Machine.
Good example of application can be the analysis of financial data from stock market. Given price of stock can be categorized in N possible intervals. Then upon observation of data with time the
S
matrix could be determined in stochastic Finite State Machine. This
S
matrix can be later converted into tight-binding model that can be represented by single-electron devices in framework of position dependent qubit polarized with time-dependent voltages. In such case one can covert the classical information into quantum information that could be later processed by quantum algorithms. One of main results presented here is equivalence of Schroedinger and tight-binding formalism in any time-dependent Hamiltonianians as given by equation
159
. This points mathematical equivalence of stochastic finite state machine and tight-binding (Wannier model) and Schroedinger model. Very last can have profound importance in quantum information processing technologies especially in reference to the semiconductor CMOS (and non-CMOS) single-electron devices that can have very high integration. This equivalence shall be not surprising as there is a lot of work on analogies of quantum mechanics and statistical physics as given by
[
34
]
,
[
23
]
and by
[
7
]
.
